### 2026-07-16: AI Gateway route candidates must include ops pause and key readiness
- Symptom: A model could be published and visible in the workspace, but task creation still failed later with no usable provider route.
- Cause: Route validation returned only the first matching provider. Disabled providers were not fully removed when no fallback existed, and OpenAI-compatible models could stop at `openai-official` even when only `toapis` had a usable key.
- Fix: Shared Gateway route resolution now exposes all executable candidates, filters disabled providers before planning, and route guards choose the first key-ready provider. Auth, HTTP, retry, and availability summary paths now use the same candidate logic.
- Regression: Cover Gemini fallback, OpenAI-compatible secondary-provider fallback, disabled single-provider routes, and admin availability status.

### 2026-07-16: AI Gateway job creation must recover stale credits reserve once
- Symptom: Bottom input text generation failed with `CREDITS_RESERVE_INVALID`; users were told to refresh before retrying.
- Cause: Unified Gateway creation reused cached `X-AC-Credits-*` headers from a previous task. Legacy AI Worker Proxy paths already refreshed once on invalid reserve, but `createAiJob` did not.
- Fix: `createAiJob` now catches `CREDITS_RESERVE_INVALID` once, clears local reserve cache, strips stale `X-AC-Credits-*` headers, and retries job creation so the server can issue a fresh reserve.
- Scope: Applies to text, image, video, and 3D Gateway jobs because they share `createAiJob`.

### 2026-07-16: Workspace model publish config must be durable
- Symptom: Admin supplier model checkboxes appeared saved, but re-entering the provider page reset them and the workspace did not show the selected models.
- Cause: `model-ops-config` was persisted only to `server/data/model-ops-config.json`. Online deployments can lose local disk state or serve reads/writes from different instances, so admin publication was not a true durable setting.
- Fix: `model-ops-config-store` now follows the same DB-first pattern as other gateway ops stores: Postgres when `DATABASE_URL` is available, disk fallback for local/dev, and auth startup ensures the table exists.
- Scope: Admin save, public `/api/model-ops-config`, workspace selectors, and gateway publication guards now read the same durable source.

### 2026-07-17: AI Gateway transient Postgres handling must include admin/observability paths
- Symptom: Online generation could still surface raw Postgres recovery errors after the main job store, key store, and settlement paths had retry guards.
- Cause: Secondary AI Gateway paths such as provider key health event queries and trend snapshot reads/writes still called Postgres directly. These paths can run near task creation, admin refreshes, or observability refreshes and leak transient database errors.
- Fix: Wrap AI Gateway provider key health listing/summary queries and trend snapshot ensure/save/list calls with `withAiGatewayPostgresRetry`. Keep auth/login DB behavior conservative; do not globally monkey patch `pg`.
- Regression: Gateway settlement tests must disable real execution when asserting reserve/finalize behavior, otherwise execution failure can immediately release the reserve and make the test look like reserve never happened.
- Follow-up: Usage billing and platform reserve settlement are part of the same generation chain. Wrap usage event ensure/insert/list and reserve acquire/release/batch settlement with the same retry helper so successful upstream generation is not marked failed during a short Postgres recovery window.

### 2026-07-17: Render Postgres outage must not fall back to local auth JSON
- Symptom: Render Postgres was resumed but stayed `unavailable`; the admin UI appeared to show only 3 users although production data still had 40 users.
- Cause: The production auth API could fall back to the local `auth-db.json` when Postgres was unavailable, which made an infrastructure outage look like user data loss. The database itself was also on a 1GB disk and remained unavailable after resume until storage was increased.
- Fix: Keep auth-api process alive for Render health checks, but return 503 for DB-backed routes while Postgres is unavailable. Disable production JSON fallback for auth data. Increase Render Postgres disk from 1GB to 5GB and enable disk autoscaling; after recovery, verify direct Postgres connection and `users` count.
- Regression: Auth startup tests should cover the production-unavailable path: the port opens, login returns 503 during the outage, and no local JSON auth store is used.
- Follow-up: For production incidents, first distinguish service health from database health. If Render Postgres is `unavailable`, check disk size/autoscaling, restart once, then use PITR only if the original database does not recover.

### 2026-07-17: AI Gateway jobs must not persist inline media payloads
- Symptom: Render Postgres recovered briefly and then returned to `unavailable`; logs showed `could not write to file "pg_wal/xlogtemp.*": No space left on device`.
- Cause: `ai_gateway_jobs` had only dozens of rows, but JSONB/TOAST storage grew close to 900MB because image jobs persisted inline base64/data URL payloads in input/output/artifact fields. This filled the 1GB database disk and caused repeated crash recovery.
- Fix: Redact inline media and very large strings before persisting AI Gateway job records, cap stored JSON payloads, and keep URLs/status/metadata needed for operations. One-time production cleanup pruned oversized job payloads and `VACUUM FULL ai_gateway_jobs` reduced the table from about 897MB to about 280KB.
- Regression: Persistent job store tests must cover both input media redaction and output/artifact media redaction.

### 2026-07-17: AI Worker Proxy handoff must tolerate Render cold starts
- Symptom: Online generation failed with `AI Worker Proxy rejected AI job handoff: HTTP 502` and a Render HTML 502 page, while `/healthz` recovered moments later.
- Cause: Auth API marked the Gateway job failed after a short transient worker outage during Render instance wakeup, deploy switch, or process restart. Fetch-level connection failures were also not retried.
- Fix: Gateway handoff now retries transient `429/502/503/504` responses and retryable fetch errors, waits for `/healthz` on worker cold-start style failures, and uses longer default handoff timeout/retry windows.
- Regression: `aiGatewayExecutor` covers transient 502 HTML and connection reset handoff recovery.

### 2026-07-18: Frontend AI job creation must not collapse network errors to "request failed"
- Symptom: Workspace image generation showed only `请求失败`; Render logs around the screenshot time showed auth-api and ai-worker-proxy were still waking up, with no matching `/api/ai/jobs` application error.
- Cause: Browser fetch failures before a JSON response were not wrapped with endpoint diagnostics. AI job creation also had no client id, so safely retrying a POST during cold starts risked duplicate jobs.
- Fix: `requestJson` now wraps network failures as `NETWORK_REQUEST_FAILED` with URL/method/detail. `createAiJob` assigns a client id when missing and retries transient network failures with the same id.
- Regression: `httpClient` covers network diagnostics; `aiJobsClient` covers client idempotency and same-id retry.

### 2026-07-18: Successful image jobs need flexible artifact URL extraction
- Symptom: An image job reached `status=succeeded` with `artifacts=1`, but the workspace still failed with `AI Gateway image job succeeded without image output`.
- Cause: The frontend only accepted narrow artifact shapes such as `{ kind: "image", url }`. Gateway/proxy/archive paths can return image-like artifacts with `mimeType=image/*` and fields such as `publicUrl`, `previewUrl`, `objectUrl`, `downloadUrl`, or Gemini `fileData.fileUri`.
- Fix: Frontend image extraction now infers image artifacts from kind/type/mime and checks standard URL aliases. Server proxy artifact extraction now supports `mime_type/contentType` and file URI artifacts.
- Regression: `runUnifiedGeneration` covers mime-typed `publicUrl` artifacts; `aiWorkerProxyUsage` covers `fileData.fileUri`.

### 2026-07-18: Retryable worker handoff failures should stay queued
- Symptom: After one successful online generation, the next image job failed again with `AI Worker Proxy rejected AI job handoff: HTTP 502` and a Render HTML page.
- Cause: The worker service can sleep or restart between jobs. Synchronous handoff retries still had a hard end, so a retryable 502 eventually marked the user job failed even though the correct state was "waiting for worker to wake".
- Fix: Retryable handoff failures now defer execution: keep the AI Gateway job queued, record the last handoff error, and schedule background handoff retries. Non-transient handoff errors still fail immediately.
- Regression: `aiGatewayExecutor` covers transient 502 deferred retry and non-transient 400 failure.

### 2026-07-19: Deferred AI Gateway handoff timers must survive Render restarts
- Symptom: Admin Generation Test and workspace jobs stayed `created`/`queued` with `startedAt=null`, no `proxyJobId`, and `gatewayExecution.lastHandoffError` showing Render 502, while ai-worker-proxy `/healthz` was healthy and idle.
- Cause: Deferred handoff retry was only an in-memory timer. On Render sleep/restart/deploy, the timer was lost, leaving persisted jobs queued with no durable worker pickup.
- Fix: auth-api now starts an AI Gateway queue recovery loop after store init. It scans recent auth-api-created `created`/`queued` jobs without `proxyJobId` and re-runs the worker handoff.
- Regression: `aiGatewayExecutor` covers recovery of a persisted queued job whose deferred handoff timer was lost.
## 2026-07-18 Gemini 3.1 Flash Image 不应默认发布为可执行

- 现象：`gemini-3.1-flash-image` 生图不稳定/不可用，但 `gemini-3-pro-image` 可正常生成；旧默认和通配 Gateway 规则会继续把 3.1 暴露给前端，导致任务进入不可用通道。
- 原因：模型目录用 `^gemini-` 通配标记 gateway_ready，且默认生图模型、快捷输入预设、无偏好纠偏路径仍指向或优先经过 3.1。
- 修复：默认生图模型改为 `gemini-3-pro-image`；`gemini-3.1-flash-image` / preview 在 Gateway 路由层标为 pending；默认模型偏好和快捷输入跟随统一默认值。
- 防线：`tests/modelRouteCatalog.test.ts` 锁定 3.1 不再 gateway_ready；`tests/modelRegistryMerge.test.ts` 锁定旧 3.1 配置会自动纠偏到 Pro。
### 2026-07-18: Route Check is not an end-to-end generation test
- Symptom: Admin supplier checks could pass while real workspace generation still failed later during upstream execution, artifact extraction, or restore.
- Cause: Key Check and Route Check intentionally do not create generation tasks, so they cannot prove the adapter returned usable text/image output.
- Fix: Add admin `POST /api/admin/model-generation-test` for text/image. It creates a minimal real AI Gateway job, waits for terminal status, verifies expected output/artifacts, and records an audit log.
- Regression: `tests/aiGatewayModelGenerationTest.test.ts` covers unsupported modalities, text success, missing image artifacts, and job creation failure.
### 2026-07-19: AI Gateway worker handoff identity must be signed and success must clear stale errors
- Symptom: Recovered/admin image Generation Test jobs could fail worker handoff with `LOGIN_REQUIRED`, and jobs that later succeeded could still show an old handoff error in admin lists.
- Cause: Auth API handoff rebuilt after recovery could no longer rely on the browser session cookie. It sent `X-AC-Fairness-Key` but not the matching HMAC signature, so strict worker settings had no durable trusted identity. Status patches also preserved `job.error` unless explicitly cleared.
- Fix: Auth API worker handoff now signs the fairness key whenever the shared HMAC secret is configured, including recovery retries. Successful proxy handoff and completed proxy polling explicitly set `error: null`; worker trace success does the same.
- Regression: `aiGatewayExecutor` covers signed handoff headers during normal and recovered jobs, plus stale error cleanup after proxy success.

### 2026-07-19: Route-ready must not mean generation-ready for unavailable upstream models
- Symptom: `gemini-3-pro-preview` passed Route Check but real Generation Test failed with Vertex 404 `Publisher model ... was not found or your project does not have access to it`.
- Cause: The shared Gemini Gateway rule matched every `gemini-*` model except the already-known 3.1 image variants, so an unavailable text preview model was advertised as Gateway executable.
- Fix: Exclude `gemini-3-pro-preview` from executable Gateway rules and expose it as `adapter_pending` until the upstream model id/location is verified.
- Regression: `modelRouteCatalog` covers that `gemini-3-pro-preview` is no longer Gateway-ready.
# 2026-07-19: Agent Platform must use provider key pool before ADC fallback
- 现象：Google Gemini/Agent Platform 生成链路容易被误判成模型 404、Vertex ADC 未配置或区域问题；后台供应商中心看得到 Google，但 key 池无法管理它。
- 原因：旧 `vertex-site` 被当成“站点代理/ADC 环境”而非供应商插头；auth-api 创建任务时没有从供应商 key 池取 Agent Platform API key，也没有把 key 随内部 ai-worker-proxy handoff 注入，worker 只能继续走 ADC 检查。
- 修复：`vertex-site` 改为 key-pool supplier plug，Gemini Gateway route 标记 `platformKeyRequired: true`；auth-api server-side acquire key 后只放入内部 worker 请求；worker 支持 `@google/genai` Vertex API-key Express Mode，并保留 ADC fallback。

# 2026-07-19: Queued AI Gateway jobs without proxyJobId must be recoverable
- 现象：工作流“线稿/生图步”前端只提示请求失败；后台任务停在 `queued`，`proxyJobId=null`，metadata 里记录 ai-worker-proxy handoff HTTP 502。
- 原因：Render ai-worker-proxy 冷启动/502 时任务进入延迟重试，但 auth-api 内存定时器可能丢失；恢复器只覆盖 `created` 或带显式 handoff error 的部分 queued 场景，仍可能漏掉 queued/no-proxy 的任务。
- 修复：恢复器对所有 `created/queued + authApiFacade + no proxyJobId + 超过最小年龄` 的任务重启 handoff；新增管理员恢复入口 `/api/admin/ai/jobs/recover-queued`，便于线上直接推进卡住任务。
### 2026-07-19: Local media asset plugs must wire import, preview, and companion suffix together
- Symptom: Generated video variants could render, but dragging a local video into the workflow did nothing; companion asset folders also had no human-readable video suffix.
- Cause: The asset model already supported `assetKind=video`, but the desktop drag/drop ingestion only accepted images and 3D model files. Companion visible filenames only covered image/text/model MIME types.
- Fix: Workflow file ingestion now accepts video files as first-class video assets, persists them through the original companion key, hydrates them back as object URLs, and companion storage emits `asset.mp4`/`asset.webm`/`asset.mov`/`asset.m4v` beside the internal `object`.
- Regression: `tests/companionAssetVisibleFile.test.ts` covers visible video suffix creation while keeping object compatibility.

### 2026-07-19: Provider routes must declare every workflow capability they can execute
- Symptom: Seedream 5.0 text-to-image worked in route planning, but bottom-input image generation with a reference image failed immediately with `AI_GATEWAY_NO_PROVIDER_ROUTE`.
- Cause: The Volcengine Ark image adapter could already serialize inline reference images, but its provider route only declared `image.generate`/`workflow_text_to_image`. Reference-image jobs are classified as `workflow_image_edit`, so the router rejected them before calling Ark.
- Fix: The Ark image provider route now declares `image.edit` and `workflow_image_edit`; the Jimeng image route also declares standard `image.edit`.
- Regression: `tests/aiGatewayServerPlan.test.ts` covers a Seedream 5.0 reference-image workflow job and verifies it still plans through `volcengine-ark-image`; `tests/aiGatewayWorkerRegistry.test.ts` now requires every implemented image route to declare the standard image workflow capabilities.

### 2026-07-19: Ark Seedream 5.0 image tests must meet upstream minimum pixels
- Symptom: Volcengine Ark key smoke and route diagnostics passed, but real generation diagnostics failed with HTTP 400 `image size must be at least 3686400 pixels`.
- Cause: Admin generation tests and default Ark image requests still sent small sizes such as `1024x1024`, while Seedream 5.0 requires at least 3,686,400 output pixels. Repeated generation failures can also put the provider key into temporary cooldown, making later checks look like "no usable platform key".
- Fix: Normalize Ark image output sizes before calling upstream. Default aspect-ratio sizes now meet the minimum, and explicit small sizes are upscaled while preserving their ratio.
- Regression: `tests/aiGatewayServerPlan.test.ts` covers explicit `1024x1024` upscaling; `tests/aiGatewayOpenAiOfficialAdapter.test.ts` covers the adapter request body. HTTP 429 Safe Experience Mode / account limit errors are account-side and should be shown as upstream account limits, not treated as router failures.

### 2026-07-19: Provider key smoke tests must prove upstream auth when a cheap probe exists
- Symptom: Volcengine Jimeng/Visual supplier credentials could look complete in the admin center, but real generation failed immediately with a generic request failure. A locally supplied AK/SK returned upstream `InvalidAccessKey`.
- Cause: The key smoke layer only checked credential shape for non-OpenAI/non-Tripo suppliers, so an invalid AK/SK could be saved as "passed" until a real generation task hit the upstream auth layer.
- Fix: Jimeng provider key smoke now sends a signed `CVSync2AsyncGetResult` probe with a dummy task id. It does not create a generation task, but it verifies Volcengine accepts the AK/SK and surfaces upstream errors such as `InvalidAccessKey` or signature failures.
- Regression: `tests/aiGatewayProviderKeyStore.test.ts` covers Jimeng real smoke success and `InvalidAccessKey` failure; `tests/jimeng.api-route.test.ts` covers Volcengine auth error surfacing from submit.

### 2026-07-20: Quick compose parameter labels must be decoded and model-scoped
- Symptom: Bottom quick-compose 3D parameters showed raw `\u...` strings, and image/model parameters could remain visible even after switching to a model that did not support them.
- Cause: Some visible JSX text nodes used escaped Unicode literals as text, which React renders literally. The image parameter panel also relied on generic options instead of the selected model's declared capability set, and stale values were not always cleared when switching models.
- Fix: Decode capability option labels defensively, replace raw visible escaped text with real labels, scope image and 3D parameter rows/summaries to `resolveModelParameterCapabilities`, and clear unsupported image/3D settings on model switch.
- Regression: Build plus provider/model catalog tests should be run after quick-compose parameter changes; add a focused UI test if this panel gets a stable test harness.

### 2026-07-20: Tripo model result extraction must not require file extensions
- Symptom: Tripo 3D jobs could finish upstream, but the workspace reported "Tripo task produced no downloadable model" and offered only old-task polling or paid resubmission.
- Cause: The workflow result parser filtered model URLs by visible file extensions such as `.glb`/`.fbx`. Tripo can return signed download URLs without extensions under semantic fields such as `model.url`, while preview images may live beside them.
- Fix: Classify Tripo artifacts by field path plus URL/MIME-like hints, keep extensionless model/download links, and separate preview images from model files in both AI Gateway and workflow-side parsing.
- Regression: `tests/tripoArtifactExtraction.test.ts` covers extensionless signed model URLs and preview/model separation.

### 2026-07-20: Video results must fall back from companion URL import to browser blob PUT
- Symptom: Generated videos could have a temporary preview URL but failed to persist locally with `cannot_normalize_media_src`, leaving the asset unpreviewable after reload/use.
- Cause: Video persistence first tried the companion `import-url` path and only fell back on HTTP 404. Other import failures, signed URLs, same-site `/api/r2` URLs, or companion fetch limitations could skip the browser fetch-and-PUT path.
- Fix: Video media persistence now falls back to browser fetch plus companion PUT after any `import-url` failure, and normalizes same-site/R2 paths with the same fetch mapping used by image persistence.
- Regression: `tests/workflowCompanionMediaPersist.test.ts` covers both old 404 import fallback and non-404 import fallback for reachable videos.

### 2026-07-20: Provider video persistence needs a server-side artifact fallback
- Symptom: Video results still failed local companion persistence when the temporary preview URL was reachable in a browser but not fetchable from frontend persistence code.
- Cause: Provider video URLs can be signed, CORS-restricted, or require provider-key proxy fetching. The workflow saved only the URL and MIME type, so the persistence layer could not use the existing `/api/ai/provider-artifacts/:provider/fetch-file` fallback.
- Fix: Carry `providerId` from AI Gateway video job results through capability execution into workflow media persistence; after companion `import-url` and browser fetch fail, fetch the video through the provider artifact proxy and PUT that blob into the local companion.
- Regression: `tests/workflowCompanionMediaPersist.test.ts` covers provider-proxy fallback when browser fetch is blocked.

### 2026-07-20: Tripo AI Gateway image-to-3D must accept referenceImages
- Symptom: Tripo 3D generation could fail despite the workflow having an input image.
- Cause: Some AI Gateway 3D callers send reference images as `input.referenceImages`, while the Tripo adapter only accepted `imageUrl` or `imageBase64DataUrl` and defaulted to `text_to_model` without checking references.
- Fix: Tripo task body creation now treats a first `referenceImages` item as `image_to_model` input when no explicit type is set, supporting both data URLs and remote image URLs.
- Regression: `tests/tripoArtifactExtraction.test.ts` covers reference-image Tripo worker request construction.

### 2026-07-20: Tripo image-to-3D references need browser-side JPEG safety before Gateway handoff
- Symptom: Online Tripo text-to-3D could submit and complete, but image-to-3D failed immediately at upload with Tripo HTTP 400 `One or more of your parameter is invalid`.
- Cause: The legacy direct Tripo path compressed/normalized reference images before upload, but the AI Gateway path could send raw canvas/project images. Very small thumbnails, PNG/WebP data URLs, or overlarge inputs could reach `/upload/sts` without the safe JPEG normalization Tripo expects.
- Fix: Tripo workflow and generic AI Gateway 3D callers now run reference images through `prepareImageDataUrlForTripoUpload`, which emits JPEG and upscales tiny inputs to a 512px max edge. Server upload also preserves the actual MIME-derived file type instead of hardcoding `jpg`, and rejects clearly tiny images with a local validation error.
- Regression: `tests/aiGatewayTripoAdapter.test.ts`, `tests/tripoArtifactExtraction.test.ts`, and `tests/aiGatewayModel3dExecution.test.ts` cover the affected adapter/request paths.

### 2026-07-20: Tripo `/upload/sts` is a credential request, not a multipart upload
- Symptom: Tripo text-to-3D succeeded online, but every image-to-3D job failed immediately with HTTP 400 `One or more of your parameter is invalid`, even after sending a normalized 512px JPEG.
- Cause: The OpenAPI adapter posted the image file directly to `/upload/sts`. Public Tripo client implementations use `/upload` for multipart image upload and return `data.image_token`; `/upload/sts` is only a temporary S3 credential fallback path.
- Fix: Tripo data URL inputs now prefer publishing the normalized reference image to public R2 and creating the task with `file: { type: "url" }`, which is the same path verified online. If R2 is unavailable, upload still posts multipart images to `/upload` first and can fall back to STS + S3.
- Regression: `tests/aiGatewayTripoAdapter.test.ts` verifies public URL bridging, direct upload, STS fallback, S3 PutObject parameters, and final `image_to_model` task body.

### 2026-07-21: AI Studio BYOK must not fall back to retired Gemini 2.5 defaults
- Symptom: Google AI Studio key validation could pass for current Gemini 3 text, while app/proxy requests failed with upstream HTTP 404 when no explicit model was supplied.
- Cause: `server/ai-worker-proxy-api.js` still defaulted omitted text models to `gemini-2.5-flash`; Google now returns `This model models/gemini-2.5-flash is no longer available to new users` for some AI Studio keys. A few direct browser image helpers also hardcoded `gemini-2.5-flash-image`.
- Fix: Proxy text fallback now uses `gemini-3-flash-preview`; direct image helpers use the registry default `DEFAULT_MODEL_IMAGE`, and multi-image generation normalizes legacy gear ids before resolving upstream.
- Regression: Check `node --check server/ai-worker-proxy-api.js`, `tests/modelRegistryResolve.test.ts`, and an online AI Studio smoke using a current Gemini 3 model when touching BYOK fallback routing.

### 2026-07-21: Gemini route order must be operational, not hard-coded
- Symptom: AI Studio upstream generation succeeded with the user's key, but online workspace jobs could still fail as if they were using the old Google/Vertex channel.
- Cause: Gemini bindings and AI Gateway executable routes listed `vertex-proxy` / `vertex-site` before `gemini-aistudio`, so when both routes were enabled and ready the system pinned the job to the static first route. Hard-coding AI Studio first would have fixed one incident but removed operator control.
- Fix: Admin model ops `bindingOverrides` now drives route priority for both browser-side `pickBinding()` and Auth API Gateway route validation. The provider center exposes route priority inputs for multi-route workspace models, and route validation skips unusable/cooling/missing keys to fall back to the next sorted route.
- Regression: `tests/pickBinding.test.ts`, `tests/aiGatewayAuthApiHandler.test.ts`, `tests/aiGatewayModelPublicationGuard.test.ts`, and `tests/aiGatewayModelAvailabilitySummary.test.ts` cover admin priority and fallback.

### 2026-07-22: GPT Image 2 online failures can be server timeout, not route readiness
- Symptom: Online `gpt-image-2` jobs failed with `The operation was aborted due to timeout`, while `gpt-image-1.5` and basic route diagnostics succeeded.
- Cause: The AI Gateway OpenAI-compatible server adapter used the generic 120s upstream timeout for all image models. GPT Image 2, especially high-resolution requests, can legitimately run longer; the browser-side OpenAI helper already treated it as a long-running model, but the server Gateway path did not.
- Fix: Server-side OpenAI-compatible execution now defaults `gpt-image-2` requests to 600s and maps `imageConfig.imageSize` tiers (`1K`/`2K`/`4K`) to concrete GPT Image 2 `WIDTHxHEIGHT` sizes before calling `/images/generations` or `/images/edits`.
- Regression: `tests/aiGatewayOpenAiOfficialAdapter.test.ts` covers GPT Image 2 4K size mapping and the 600s timeout; online diagnostics should still pass for both `gpt-image-2` and `gpt-image-1.5`.

### 2026-07-22: Long AI Gateway jobs must not block the create-job response
- Symptom: Online `gpt-image-2` generation could succeed on the server, but the workspace still showed "请求失败" because the initial `POST /api/ai/jobs` request waited for the entire image generation.
- Cause: Auth API job creation synchronously awaited gateway execution in production. Long image models can take more than two minutes, so browser/proxy request timeouts can fire before the completed job is available through polling.
- Fix: Production Auth API creation now returns a queued AI Gateway job immediately and starts execution in the background; tests can still opt into synchronous behavior, and `AI_GATEWAY_CREATE_AWAIT_EXECUTION` remains available for explicit overrides.
- Regression: `tests/aiGatewayExecutor.test.ts` covers immediate queued creation while background execution starts; online verification should confirm `POST /api/ai/jobs` returns quickly for `gpt-image-2` and the job later reaches a terminal state through polling.

### 2026-07-22: Jimeng image jobs can succeed with redacted inline output
- Symptom: Jimeng image jobs reached `succeeded` with one image artifact, but the workspace showed `AI Gateway image job succeeded without image output`; job details contained `output.images` and `artifacts[0].url` as `[REDACTED_MEDIA:...]`.
- Cause: Jimeng Visual returns bare base64 image strings rather than `data:image/...;base64,...` data URLs. The AI Gateway media archiver only uploaded data URLs to R2, so bare base64 survived until the persistence sanitizer redacted it.
- Fix: The AI Gateway media archiver now detects large bare base64 image payloads, uploads them once to R2, and reuses that URL across `output` and `artifacts`; the frontend image extractor also accepts URL arrays in `output.images`.
- Regression: `tests/aiGatewayJobMediaArchive.test.ts` covers Jimeng bare base64 archival and dedupe; `tests/runUnifiedGeneration.test.ts` covers `output.images[]` URL extraction.

### 2026-07-22: Workflow Tripo 3D must not be hijacked by stale local BYOK
- Symptom: Dragging an image onto a Tripo 3D capability preset could fail immediately even though platform AI Gateway Tripo jobs succeeded online.
- Cause: The workflow Tripo branch preferred a locally saved Tripo API key when present, so stale or invalid BYOK settings sent the run through the legacy `/api/tripo` proxy instead of the operator-managed AI Gateway route.
- Fix: Workflow Tripo 3D submissions now use the platform AI Gateway route consistently; local Tripo keys remain useful for explicit artifact pull/fallback tools, but capability-block execution no longer depends on them. Normal reruns also create a fresh AI Gateway job instead of silently resuming stale task metadata; only the explicit "resume old task" action reuses an existing id.
- Regression: `tests/tripoWorkflowResume.test.ts` covers stale-task metadata not blocking fresh submissions; run 3D/Tripo adapter tests and an online `model3d.generate` job through AI Gateway after changing workflow 3D routing.

### 2026-07-22: Workflow Tripo preflight and artifact parsing must match AI Gateway
- Symptom: The workspace log could show `Tripo task produced no downloadable model`, and provider-key recent events could stay empty if the run failed before AI Gateway job creation.
- Cause: The workflow credit preflight still treated `workflow_generate_3d` as a generic job-kind check, allowing local Tencent/Tripo credential state to influence the Tripo platform path before `POST /api/ai/jobs`. The frontend Tripo result adapter also only accepted a narrow artifact shape.
- Fix: Tripo and Ark 3D workflow presets now preflight as platform Gateway jobs regardless of stale local BYOK state; Tencent keeps its credential bypass. Tripo polling now accepts `output.model_url` / `rendered_image_url` and model links from AI Gateway artifacts using `url`, `publicUrl`, `signedUrl`, `href`, or `downloadUrl`.
- Regression: `tests/aiBillingGate.test.ts` locks the Tripo platform preflight, and `tests/tripoWorkflowResume.test.ts` covers sparse AI Gateway artifacts plus current Tripo output field names.

### 2026-07-26: 预览恢复了但「打开资产文件夹」仍灰色
- 现象：伴侣打开工作台、资产能显示，右键「打开资产文件夹」禁用。
- 原因：预览走 `getAssetDisplayImage`（R2/blob/内存回退），灰态只认**当前 displayKey** 的 companion key；多真相竞争导致「看得见 ≠ 可 reveal」。
- 修复：`resolveWorkflowAssetLocalHandle` 支持同资产目录回退；R2 pack 不再删除 `*CompanionKey`；companion `workflow.json` 作为本地画布真相，IDB 为 cache。
- 涉及：`services/workflowMediaLocator.ts` `services/workflowMediaRef.ts` `services/workspaceR2ImageBundle.ts` `App.tsx` `local-companion/src/storage/workflowStore.ts`
- 回归：`tests/workflowMediaLocator.test.ts` `tests/workspaceR2PreserveCompanionKeys.test.ts`

### 2026-07-26: companion workflow.json 恢复可能覆盖较新 IDB
- 现象：审查发现打开项目时稀疏/过期伴侣快照可能清空画布；快速切项目会写错 bundle；与云 merge 竞态。
- 原因：`preferCompanionWorkflowBundle` 只要 companion 有 pending/assets 就整包替换；async 无项目守卫；云 merge 与伴侣 prefer 并行。
- 修复：prefer 增加资产数量与 updatedAt 防护（pending-only 不抹 assets）；打开路径加 `activeWorkspaceProjectIdRef` 守卫；云 merge 开启时不做 companion prefer（merge 后 PUT 对齐）；autosave PUT 使用分镜恢复后的 assets。
- 涉及：`services/workflowCompanionProjectSync.ts` `App.tsx` `tests/workflowCompanionProjectSync.test.ts`

### 2026-07-26: 本地伴侣在也会丢 3D，只剩预览图
- 现象：本地伴侣已连接，打开/刷新项目后 3D 模型没了，卡片仍显示预览图。
- 原因：打开项目时 `App.tsx` 对 manifest 缺口 **fire-and-forget 修复**，随即用**修复前的 gaps** 调用 `removeMissingCompanionKeyReferences` 并写入 IDB。预览常来自 R2/`results` 或已进 manifest 的结果键得以保留；`modelCompanionKeys` / `stepModelCompanionKeys` 被清掉后无法 hydrate。模型修复还依赖内存 `modelUrls`，而落盘瘦身后该槽常为空，修复几乎必跳过。
- 修复：新增 `resolveCompanionManifestGapsForProjectOpen`——先 await 修复 → 再 reconcile/刷新 manifest → 仅清理「仍不在 manifest 且磁盘也无文件」的键；模型缺口若磁盘已有对象视为已修复。
- 涉及：`App.tsx` `services/workflowManifestCrossCheck.ts` `tests/resolveCompanionManifestGapsForProjectOpen.test.ts`
- 回归：`npx vitest run tests/resolveCompanionManifestGapsForProjectOpen.test.ts`

### 2026-07-26: 导入 3D 预览变成「本地预览」占位图
- 现象：导入 `cloth.fbx` 等模型后，卡片预览是深蓝底 +「3D 模型 / 本地预览 / 文件名」SVG，不是模型截图。
- 原因：`addModelsFromFiles` 并行跑「伴侣落盘并 revoke blob」与「离屏截图」；截图队列慢时 blob 已被 revoke，`FBXLoader` 失败，SVG placeholder 留在 `original`；卡片又把 SVG 当 `displaySrc`。
- 修复：先 `captureWorkflowModelThumbnailDataUrl(blob)` 再 put/revoke；失败则用伴侣 objectUrl 重试；卡片不把 `image/svg+xml` 当真实缩略图展示。
- 涉及：`components/WorkflowSection.tsx` `components/workflow/AssetCardPreviewRenderer.tsx`
- 回归：`tests/assetCardPreviewRenderer.test.tsx`

### 2026-07-26: 生成图画得见但「打开资产文件夹」仍灰
- 现象：AI 生成图可预览，右键打开文件夹禁用。
- 原因：落盘依赖 `assetsRef` 上 `lastKey` 与 result 字符串全等；偶发对不上则跳过 `scheduleCompanionPersistResult`；缺伴侣/项目时静默 return。
- 修复：在 `setAssets` 内写出 `persistedResultKey` 后直接 schedule；无伴侣时打 warn 日志。
- 涉及：`components/WorkflowSection.tsx`

### 2026-07-26: 文生图后图能力不可用 / 拆卡方向纠偏
- 现象：文字卡文生图后拖线稿失败或拆卡后秒失败；与「一张卡多模态、认当前显示」产品模型冲突。
- 原因：曾按出生壳禁 text.results / 写时拆卡；读路径用 `assetKind===text` 一票否决；HTTPS 落盘缺 import-url。
- 修复（收紧）：**显示态=displayKey 槽模态**；TTI 同卡写 `results`+`scheduleCompanionPersistResult`；删 derivation/migrate；门禁/预览/execute/result 水合跟当前槽；https→伴侣 import-url。
- 涉及：`services/workflowTextAsset.ts` `components/WorkflowSection.tsx` `services/workflowCompanionAssets.ts`
- 回归：`npx vitest run tests/workflowTextAsset.test.ts tests/workflowOriginalImageImportUrl.test.ts`

### 2026-07-26: P0 审查 — 旁路开项目 / 探测失败清键 / boot 未盖全
- 现象：回收站恢复可能空画布且卡住 boot；伴侣短暂超时会清 `*CompanionKey`；慢 GET 期间切项目/Agent CLI 仍可固化未 prefer 的 IDB；云 merge 未完成就解锁 autosave。
- 原因：`restoreWorkspaceTrashProject` 只读 IDB；`companionAssetKeyPresentOnDisk` 把 meta `ok:false` 当缺文件；门控未挂到 Agent CLI/回壳/切项目；`markCompanionBootReady` 不等 cloud merge。
- 修复：恢复走 `loadWorkspaceProjectInternal`；磁盘探测三态 present/absent/unknown（unknown 不 clean）；旁路 save 复用 `isWorkflowProjectAutosaveAllowed`；companion + cloud merge 双相位都完成才 unlock。
- 涉及：`App.tsx` `services/workflowManifestCrossCheck.ts` `tests/resolveCompanionManifestGapsForProjectOpen.test.ts`
- 回归：`npx vitest run tests/resolveCompanionManifestGapsForProjectOpen.test.ts tests/workflowProjectBootBarrier.test.ts`

### 2026-07-26: 伴侣资产命名改为 full/thumb + 短 ID
- 现象：旧键 `result-{超长 actionId}` / `image-N` 难扫盘、易超长；缩略图未稳定挂键。
- 原因：命名未收口；manifest 合并只认 `wf-*` / `result-*` / `model-N`。
- 修复：新写统一 `{assetId}/{mediaKind}-{role}-{slot}-{id8}.{ext}`（role 仅 full/thumb）；结果必写 full + best-effort thumb → `resultsCompanionKeys` / `resultsPreviewCompanionKeys`；旧键仅读兼容；扫盘 slot0=`original`、slot>0=`slot_N`。
- 涉及：`services/workflowCompanionAssets.ts` `services/workflowManifestCrossCheck.ts` `components/WorkflowSection.tsx` `types.ts`
- 回归：`npx vitest run tests/workflowImageCompanionNaming.test.ts tests/workflowManifestMerge.test.ts`

### 2026-07-24: 工作台打不开（3000 挂掉 / 错端口）
- 现象：工作台黑屏或完全打不开。
- 原因：配置端口是 `3000`，但本机只剩错误端口 `5173` 上的 Vite；`3000` 无监听。另有多组 `concurrently` 残留，端口混乱。本次 `WorkflowSection` 模块本身非空（~2.4MB，有 `default`），不同于 07-10 空模块黑屏。
- 修复：清 `node_modules/.vite`；`App.tsx` 懒加载改指 `workflowSectionLazyBoot0724`；执行 `npm run restart:local-stack` 拉起 `http://localhost:3000/`。
- 涉及：`App.tsx` `components/workflow/workflowSectionLazyBoot0724.ts` `scripts/restart-local-stack.ps1`

### 2026-08-03: Codex one-click must not keep stale localhost auth
- Symptom: Desktop one-click Codex setup could pass CLI and conversation checks but fail cloud identity with `net::ERR_CONNECTION_REFUSED` because old settings kept `http://127.0.0.1:9100/api/team/codex/auth`.
- Cause: setup reused an existing `codexSharedAuthUrl`; the default auth origin could also resolve from old shell settings to localhost, so the "default" was still not a cloud route.
- Fix: `defaultCodexSharedAuthUrl()` now falls back to production auth-api when the resolved origin is localhost unless explicitly overridden by `COMPANION_AUTH_API_ORIGIN`; one-click setup replaces localhost/invalid shared-auth URLs with that cloud default.
- Regression: `tests/copilotSettingsUi.test.ts` checks for the replacement helper and production fallback; unpacked desktop launch now rewrites the URL to `https://assetcutter-auth-api.onrender.com/api/team/codex/auth` and fails with 401 when not logged in, instead of connection refused.

### 2026-08-03: Launch-arg Codex setup must recover from Workbench login
- Symptom: Installer/shortcut-triggered `--assetcutter-codex-one-click-setup` could stop at `http_401` even though the Copilot panel button had a login recovery flow.
- Cause: launch-arg setup ran in the main process and did not reuse the renderer's Workbench sign-in wait/retry path.
- Fix: launch-arg setup now opens the Workbench view, waits for the shell team account to become logged in, then retries Codex one-click setup automatically.
- Regression: `tests/copilotSettingsUi.test.ts` checks for `openWorkbenchForCodexLaunchLogin`, `waitForShellLoginForCodexLaunch`, and `launch_retry_after_login`; app.asar inspection confirmed these strings in the 20260803-212904 package.

### 2026-08-03: Persisted Codex setup reports must be encoding-stable
- Symptom: An older installed desktop build wrote `codexLastSetupReport` with mojibake Chinese strings that broke `settings.json`, causing clean-machine verification to fail before reaching the real cloud identity gate.
- Cause: user-visible localized report text was persisted into the long-lived agent settings file, where Windows/packaging encoding mismatches could corrupt string literals.
- Fix: setup report labels/details/next actions persisted by `companion-desktop/main.cjs` are now ASCII-only; UI can localize around them. `agent-store.cjs` also normalizes `codexLastSetupReport` shape before writing.
- Regression: unpacked 0.2.12 launch confirmed `settings.json` parses and report labels are `Local settings|Local tool channel|Cloud identity|Codex CLI|Conversation engine|Conversation verification`.

### 2026-08-03: Clean-machine PS1 must tolerate Codex stderr warnings
- Symptom: `verify-codex-clean-machine.ps1` could abort during Codex real conversation smoke because Codex prints a PowerShell shell-snapshot warning to stderr.
- Cause: `$ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"` converted native stderr warning output into a terminating script error.
- Fix: the Codex smoke section temporarily uses `ErrorActionPreference = "Continue"` while still capturing `2>&1` and checking the actual Codex exit/text result.
- Regression: PS1 parse passes and smoke proceeds past the warning in local short acceptance runs.

### 2026-08-03: Codex auth route must validate payload before 200
- Symptom: `/api/team/codex/auth` could return 200 whenever env existed, even if `CODEX_SHARED_AUTH_JSON_BASE64` was not decodable JSON. Desktop would then fail later while writing `auth.json`.
- Cause: auth-api checked presence of shared auth env but not parseability.
- Fix: auth-api now validates that `authJsonBase64` or `authJson` resolves to a JSON object; invalid config returns 503 `invalid_codex_shared_auth`. Production verification also parses the logged-in payload.
- Regression: `tests/verifyCodexProductionPayload.test.ts` starts a local mock auth-api and confirms a 200 route with invalid payload fails `verify-codex-one-click-production.mjs`.

### 2026-08-03: Finalizer must separate admin and auth-check cookies
- Symptom: The Codex production finalizer used one `cookie` variable for both desktop artifact publishing and `/api/team/codex/auth` payload verification.
- Cause: admin registration and team-session identity checks were treated as the same credential source.
- Fix: finalizer now uses `adminCookie` for publishing and `authCheckCookie` for payload verification, preferring `CODEX_SHARED_AUTH_CHECK_COOKIE` for the latter.
- Regression: `tests/copilotSettingsUi.test.ts` checks for `authCheckCookie` and `CODEX_SHARED_AUTH_CHECK_COOKIE`; local finalizer dry-run prints both cookie states separately.

### 2026-08-03: Validate Codex shared-auth env before Render paste
- Symptom: Admins could export `.env.codex-shared-auth.local` but had no local command to validate the value before pasting it into Render.
- Cause: validation existed at desktop sync time and now auth-api route time, but not at the operator handoff point.
- Fix: added `scripts/validate-codex-shared-auth-env.mjs` and `npm run companion:codex-auth-env:check`; finalizer runs it when shared auth env is present.
- Regression: `tests/validateCodexSharedAuthEnv.test.ts` covers valid base64 auth JSON and malformed env values.

### 2026-08-03: Clean-machine PS1 must validate Codex auth payload content
- Symptom: The no-repo Windows acceptance script could mark the logged-in Codex identity check OK on HTTP 200 even if the response body did not decode to a usable Codex `auth.json` object.
- Cause: `verify-codex-clean-machine.ps1` checked only response status, while the Node production verifier already parsed `authJsonBase64` / `authJson`.
- Fix: added `Test-CodexAuthPayload` to parse JSON, decode `authJsonBase64` when present, and fail invalid or non-object payloads.
- Regression: `tests/copilotSettingsUi.test.ts` now guards the PS1 payload parser strings, and PowerShell scriptblock parsing passes.

### 2026-08-03: Codex finalizer must require clean-machine evidence
- Symptom: The finalizer could continue through local packaging and production route checks even when no fresh Windows machine had proven one-click setup, cloud identity sync, and a real Codex reply.
- Cause: clean-machine acceptance existed as a separate script, but the production finalizer did not consume its report as a release gate.
- Fix: `scripts/finalize-codex-one-click-production.mjs` now supports `--require-clean-machine --clean-machine-report=<report.json>` and validates the report's version, one-click setup flags, logged-in auth payload, update feed, and conversation smoke before continuing.
- Regression: `tests/finalizeCodexOneClickProduction.test.ts` verifies the finalizer stops before manifest/publish checks when clean-machine evidence is required but missing.

### 2026-08-03: Desktop manifest generation and publish dry-run must be serial
- Symptom: Running `companion:desktop-upload-manifest` and `companion:desktop-publish -- --dry-run` in parallel can make dry-run publish read the previous `desktop-upload-manifest.json`, reporting an older installer even though a newer package was just built.
- Cause: both commands share the same manifest path, and publish can read before the manifest writer finishes.
- Fix: run manifest generation first, wait for it to finish, then run publish/dry-run. Finalizer already runs these commands serially.
- Regression: after rebuilding `AssetCutterCompanion-0.2.12-20260803-224701-x64.exe`, rerunning dry-run publish serially reported the matching `224701` file and R2 key.

### 2026-08-06: Copilot tool creation must not become a Workbench text asset
- Symptom: Asking Copilot to create a Maya plugin/tool produced a Workbench text asset containing code and instructions.
- Cause: The Copilot/Codex prompt and MCP tool catalog emphasized `ac.workbench.create_text_asset` for generated text, but did not clearly route tool/plugin/script-tool creation to the shell tool authoring chain.
- Fix: Codex prompts, MCP quickstart, tool catalog, and workbench-flow now point tool/plugin creation to `ac.shell_tool.scaffold -> ac.shell_tool.authored_upsert`; `createTextAsset` rejects obvious plugin/tool code payloads before touching the Workbench asset list.
- Regression: `tests/agentWorkbenchClient.test.ts`, `tests/agentToolSchema.test.ts`, and `tests/codexBrainAdapter.test.ts` cover the misroute guard and routing guidance.

### 2026-08-07: Host bridge probes must not leak English/internal errors
- Symptom: Copilot-created host drafts and cloud host versions could show probe/install messages such as `heartbeat file was not found` or `target_dir_not_allowed`.
- Cause: Shared bridge templates and draft/cloud install wrappers returned low-level probe text directly to the Host Center UI.
- Fix: Template probes now return Chinese, action-oriented messages for HTTP health, heartbeat, and command-port states; draft/cloud install wrappers translate protected-directory and path-boundary failures.
- Regression: `tests/hostBridgeDrafts.test.ts` checks Chinese heartbeat and protected-directory correction messages; `tests/hostBridgeTemplates.test.ts` keeps template coverage green.

### 2026-08-09: Host acceptance gate buttons must preserve object-session intent
- Symptom: A missing acceptance group could render as a jump button but fail to open the host-specific Copilot acceptance guide.
- Cause: The hand-built HTML attribute string around `data-bridge-jump` / `data-bridge-acceptance-guide` was quote-sensitive, and the click binding did not pass the guide flag to `jumpToBridgeCard`.
- Fix: Build the button attributes with complete quoted attributes and pass `{ acceptanceGuide }` into `jumpToBridgeCard`, which opens the host object Copilot session with an acceptance prompt.
- Regression: `tests/shellHostCenterUi.test.ts` now checks the guide attribute, click binding, object Copilot session call, and prompt handoff.

### 2026-08-09: Running-host discovery paths must survive bad persisted labels
- Symptom: Host Center could identify already-open software and save its exe path, but later "启动宿主" still reported that the software path could not be found.
- Cause: `custom-host-targets.json` had corrupted localized `label` strings, making the JSON invalid; `readCustomHostTargets()` returned an empty store, so saved running exe paths were ignored. The UI could also send a script/plugin install target id as `targetId`, causing launch resolution to stop before falling back to saved exe paths.
- Fix: `readCustomHostTargets()` now recovers usable paths from a corrupted custom-target file; running-host discovery writes an ASCII-stable label; launch resolution falls back to all saved/running/search paths when a received target id is not a launchable saved target.
- Regression: `tests/hostAppProcess.test.ts` covers corrupted custom-target recovery and fallback from an install target id to a saved running executable path.

### 2026-08-10: Copilot host install targetDir must map to built-in host fields
- Symptom: Copilot acceptance guidance could repeatedly fail `ac.companion.host_bridge.install` even when it passed a real script/plugin/project directory, for example ZBrush returned `no_zbrush_scripts_dir` for valid ZBrush directories.
- Cause: The Copilot tool schema exposes one simple `targetDir`, but built-in host routes expect host-specific fields such as `scriptsDirs`, `projectDirs`, `pluginDirs`, `startupDirs`, `prefsDirs`, or `userDirs`.
- Fix: `agent-body-host.cjs` maps `targetDir` to the correct built-in host install field before calling `/v1/bridges/:id/install`; the ZBrush HTTP route also accepts `targetDir` directly as a fallback.
- Regression: `tests/agentToolSchema.test.ts` checks the install-field mapping, `tests/shellHostCenterUi.test.ts` checks ZBrush `targetDir` route compatibility, and a live local smoke confirmed `POST /v1/bridges/zbrush/install` with only `targetDir` writes the bridge script.

### 2026-08-10: Adobe roaming root must not be treated as a Photoshop scripts folder
- Symptom: Photoshop bridge install reported success, but Photoshop showed no plugin/script entry because the JSX was written directly under `%APPDATA%\Adobe`.
- Cause: Photoshop discovery added the broad Adobe roaming root as an install target, and stale `assetcutter_photoshop_bridge.jsx` files could later be mistaken for version candidates.
- Fix: Photoshop/Illustrator discovery now only creates menu-script targets from real version directories such as `Adobe Photoshop 2025\Presets\Scripts`; selecting the broad Adobe root maps to the newest matching version folder. Photoshop/Illustrator script names are menu-friendly (`AssetCutter Photoshop Bridge.jsx`) while uninstall/status still tolerate legacy underscore files.
- Regression: `tests/hostBridgeInstallers.test.ts` covers broad Adobe-root mapping and stale JSX filtering; `tests/shellHostCenterUi.test.ts` checks the menu-friendly Photoshop script name.

### 2026-08-11: Copilot perception bar must attach to the real shell Copilot
- Symptom: Runtime perception existed in the project Agent / QuickCompose path, but the visible right-side `Copilot codex` panel still showed no perception bar.
- Cause: The implementation followed similarly named internal Dock components instead of anchoring to the actual `#shell-copilot` DOM used by the local companion shell.
- Fix: Add `data-shell-copilot-perception-bar` directly under the local shell Copilot header and refresh it from Workbench context, object session, external connection summaries, and recent executions.
- Regression: `tests/copilotSettingsUi.test.ts` now checks the shell DOM marker and shell refresh functions so the feature cannot silently drift back into the internal Dock.

### 2026-08-11: 软件连接不能靠生命周期硬编码扩展

- Symptom: 新增 Maya、Unreal 等连接时，连接草稿能创建，但安装/探测容易卡住；后续每加一个软件都需要工程师再改一次主流程。
- Cause: 真实 bridge install/probe/uninstall 能力散落在具体软件分支、前端兜底识别和旧 Host Center 逻辑里，`capabilityLifecycle.ts` 承担了不该承担的软件识别责任。
- Fix: 软件连接扩展收口到 `softwareBridgeRegistry + SoftwareBridgeDriver`。生命周期主流程只调用 registry 匹配到的 driver；连接页消费后端 `connectionState`，不再维护本地软件支持表；Copilot prompt/schema 明确未支持软件进入 `template_missing` 并生成 driver/template 计划。
- Regression: `tests/capabilityPackages.test.ts` 用 fake driver 验证新增软件无需修改 lifecycle 主流程，并断言 `capabilityLifecycle.ts` 不包含具体软件 bridge 分支；`tests/shellConnectionPageUi.test.ts` 禁止 driver 关键词回流到连接页本地逻辑；`tests/agentToolSchema.test.ts` 和 `tests/codexBrainAdapter.test.ts` 固定 Copilot 的 registry 路由约束。

### 2026-08-11: Unreal bridge 不应只允许项目目录

- Symptom: 安装 Unreal 连接时只能选择包含 `.uproject` 的项目目录，选择 Unreal Engine 安装目录会被拒绝，表现得像路径找不到。
- Cause: Unreal bridge installer 只实现了 project plugin 目标，把项目目录当成唯一合法安装目标。
- Fix: `installUnrealBridge()` 同时支持项目目录和引擎安装目录。项目模式写入 `<Project>/Plugins/AssetCutterBridge`；引擎模式写入 `<EngineRoot>/Engine/Plugins/Marketplace/AssetCutterBridge`。手动目标类型新增 `engine_dir`。
- Regression: `tests/hostBridgeInstallers.test.ts` 覆盖 Unreal 项目目录、引擎目录安装/卸载，以及无效目录拒绝；`tests/hostBridgeCatalogConsistency.test.ts` 确认新增目标类型不破坏宿主定义一致性。

### 2026-08-11: 样例软件不能成为连接系统的世界中心

- Symptom: 用户创建未知软件连接时，系统容易先寻找 Photoshop/Maya/Blender/Unreal 等已知宿主或要求选择模板；策略失败后也容易表现为“没有支持”而不是继续探索。
- Cause: 产品心智仍残留“已知宿主列表优先”，prompt、schema、发布门禁没有把 facts、StrategyDraft、candidateStrategies 和 verified strategy 的边界锁住。
- Fix: Copilot prompt/schema 改成 unknown-first：创建连接先生成 `software_connection`、收集 `connectionFacts`、生成 `StrategyDraft/candidateStrategies`；已知 driver 只是 verified strategy shortcut。发布门禁新增 `verified_strategy_recorded`，真实 probe 成功但没有 verified strategy id 也不能提交云端。
- Regression: `tests/codexBrainAdapter.test.ts` 和 `tests/agentToolSchema.test.ts` 固定 unknown-first 提示；`tests/capabilityPackages.test.ts` 验证没有 verified strategy id 的软件连接不能发布，云端版本只保存 verified strategy id 而不带本地探索事件。

### 2026-08-11: 生命周期 HTTP 入口必须透传当前策略 id

- Symptom: 通过 HTTP 生命周期入口请求 Maya `currentStrategyId=maya-command-port`，真实 probe 成功后却记录成默认 verified strategy `maya-script-folder`。
- Cause: 底层 `runCapabilityLifecycle()` 和 Copilot 工具体已经支持 `currentStrategyId`，但 `/v1/capability-packages/:id/lifecycle` 没有从请求体透传该字段。
- Fix: `local-companion/src/httpHandler.ts` 在 lifecycle input 中补齐 `currentStrategyId`，让真实 probe 沉淀用户/agent 当前执行的策略，而不是 driver 的第一个 verified strategy。
- Regression: `tests/capabilityPackages.test.ts` 新增 HTTP handler 透传断言；本机真实 Maya2022 Command Port probe 验证 `lastProbe.verifiedStrategyId=maya-command-port`，管理员发布门禁候选也保存同一策略 id。

### 2026-08-11: 连接卡片不能把诊断字段当主 UI

- Symptom: 连接页卡片同时外露 facts、策略、阻塞、能力、失败事件和多排动作，用户要先理解系统内部状态才能判断下一步。
- Cause: 为了验证 unknown-first 架构，把排错证据直接放到了普通卡片主区域，混淆了“用户操作面”和“开发诊断面”。
- Fix: 主卡重排为应用标识、状态、软件版本、启动位置、下一步任务和主动作；策略、facts、阻塞、能力、事件日志压缩成“维护摘要”芯片，低频动作进入轻量操作区，不再用粗糙折叠块承载复杂度。
- Regression: `tests/shellConnectionPageUi.test.ts` 验证主卡具备产品化信息层级、维护摘要和轻量操作区，并断言不再出现“连接详情/更多”的折叠 details。

### 2026-08-11: 本机版本选择必须贯穿 HTTP、lifecycle 和 driver

- Symptom: 连接页版本抽屉可以选择某个本机软件版本，但启动/探测/安装仍可能使用 manifest 顶层路径或默认路径。
- Cause: 前端只把 `localVersionId` 当 UI 状态，HTTP lifecycle input、旧 install/probe/uninstall 入口和 `runCapabilityLifecycle()` 没有统一解析 `manifest.localVersions`。
- Fix: lifecycle input 增加 `localVersionId/localVersion`；调度器按所选本机版本补齐 `executablePath/targetDir` 后再调用 driver 或进程启动；HTTP 入口透传 `localVersionId`，旧 `probe` 入口不再丢弃请求体。
- Regression: `tests/capabilityPackages.test.ts` 验证 fake driver 的 install/probe/uninstall 都收到所选版本路径；`tests/shellConnectionPageUi.test.ts` 验证版本抽屉启动请求携带 `localVersionId`。
